- **The automated QA release path is exercised end to end — `v0.1.1-qa.1` published and qa.1 safely retired** (GitHub Releases, Release workflow run `33336564062`). The annotated `v0.1.1-qa.1` tag points at merged `main` commit `a7ffe56`; the tag-triggered workflow completed its full gate in 2m50s (**905 Python passed**, **95 Vitest passed**, production bundle byte-fresh, **11 Playwright passed** under the production CSP), built `enfolded-0.1.1rc1-py3-none-any.whl` once, installed and smoke-tested that exact file, then published it with SHA-256 `f70820c5f6b53acc4216d75e41949293d9501b61b2c362869f8fe0c2c71a974b`. The attached wheel was downloaded independently, matched `SHA256SUMS`, installed as `enfolded-0.1.1rc1`, and passed the installed-artifact smoke again. Historical `v0.1.0-qa.1` is now titled superseded, links to its successor, and defaults to an isolated `qa-home` in both the release body and replaced `QA_BUILD.md`; its original tag target, wheel, `requirements.lock`, and `SHA256SUMS` asset identities and digests remain unchanged. This closes and supersedes the **Outstanding, owner-applied** status recorded in the immediately following entry. **Irreversibility check:** the new QA tag/release is a deliberate durable distribution record; no migration, golden re-pin, chronicle write path, world metadata, source code, or packaged world bytes changed in this documentation follow-up.

- **QA releases carry their prerelease identity and build themselves** (`pyproject.toml`, `.github/workflows/release.yml` (new), `scripts/verify_wheel.py`). Follow-ups from the v0.1.0-qa.1 release review, applied so every future build inherits them. The package version advances to `0.1.1rc1` — two surprises drove that number. First, qa.1's hand-built wheel was plain `enfolded-0.1.0`: to pip the *same version* as the eventual launch release, so a tester with the QA wheel installed would never be offered the real thing. Second (owner review catch), the obvious fix `0.1.0rc1` orders *backward*: PEP 440 places a release candidate before its release (`0.1.0rc1 < 0.1.0`), so it would neither upgrade existing qa.1 installs nor leave the launch version newer — the published qa.1 artifact burned `0.1.0`, and the line moves to `0.1.1` (QA build N ships `0.1.1rcN`, launch ships plain `0.1.1`). The new tag-triggered Release workflow replaces qa.1's hand-built, hand-uploaded assets. Because a tag can point at any commit, the workflow assumes branch CI never saw it and runs the FULL gate itself before packaging: Ruff, full pytest (under explicit pipefail so `tee` cannot mask a red suite), Vitest, the production frontend build with the committed-bundle freshness check (the wheel packages the committed `static/app` — a stale bundle would ship stale frontend bytes), and the Playwright browser smoke under production CSP. It then builds ONE wheel and smoke-tests that exact artifact (`verify_wheel.py` gained an optional wheel-path argument; no-arg keeps the CI/check.sh throwaway build), checksums it, and publishes with machine-generated provenance notes naming the workflow run — verified bytes and published bytes are the same file. Version identity is one-to-one and enforced: a QA tag `v<base>-qa.<n>` requires package version `<base>rc<n>` exactly, a stable tag `v<x>` requires stable `<x>` exactly, rc versions publish only through QA tags, and every mismatch fails the run with an actionable error (all tag/version combinations exercised against the extracted logic). The generated install notes make DB isolation the *default* (`HOME="$PWD/qa-home"`), not a caveat: this repo has already shipped a test that polluted a real `~/.nested-worlds/worlds.db`, and a QA run must never touch a personal world. qa.1 itself is deliberately untouched — its checksums are its identity. **Outstanding, owner-applied:** the live qa.1 release body/`QA_BUILD.md` still carries the un-isolated run instructions; this session's GitHub credentials cannot edit releases (PATCH returns 403), so the prepared replacement text is posted on PR #82 for the owner to paste — this entry records the correction as pending, not done. Verified: Ruff green; **905 Python passed**; installed-wheel smoke green in both modes (no-arg temp build, and explicit-path against a prebuilt `enfolded-0.1.1rc1-py3-none-any.whl`); release-kind logic exercised across 8 tag/version combinations (2 accepts, 6 refusals). **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, golden re-pin, chronicle write path, or world_meta pin; version metadata and CI only.

- **PR #81 follow-up closes the fourth review round** (`persistence/__init__.py`, `merge_patch.py` (new), `multiverse/utils.py`, `frontend/src/components/Wayback.jsx`, regression coverage, ADR-003/009). Three new Claude findings bring the actionable inline total to 25. The double-gated mutation prune now repairs an invalid rollback marker, folds the deleted material prefix into `legacy_baseline`, preserves the node-version high-water mark, rebuilds and re-marks the cache in the same transaction, and refuses timestamp-disordered non-prefix deletion rather than silently changing state. A failed React Wayback fetch now clears its stale snapshot and actively returns any archive audio preview to the present. Python persistence and multiverse hydration now share one RFC 7396 implementation instead of maintaining recursive copies. Verified: Ruff green; **905 Python passed**; **95 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **11 Playwright E2E passed** under production CSP, including the active-listen failure regression. **Irreversibility check remains TRIPPED — awaiting owner confirmation before merge.** No further migration, history rewrite, golden re-pin, new chronicle kind, or new write path; this hardens the already-declared 0016–0017 rollback contract.

- **The archive becomes experiential — Wayback renders the world at any recorded moment through its present senses (pre-launch batch 4, ADR-011 ratified)** (`persistence/__init__.py` (`get_wayback_state`, born-relative merge-patch cache rebuild), `persistence/migrations/{0016_property_cache_meta,0017_property_cache_delta_version}.sql` (rollback-compatible cache metadata), `server/handlers.py` (`GET /wayback`), `multiverse/utils.py` / `server/world_mechanics.py` / `puzzles/gates.py` (tombstone-correct overlay hydration), `static/clientlogic.js`, `static/explorer.js`, `static/index.html`, `static/nodesound.js`, `frontend/src/wayback.js`, `frontend/src/components/Wayback.jsx`, `frontend/src/components/TextPanel.jsx`, `frontend/src/App.jsx`, production bundle, `tests/test_wayback.py` (new, 7 tests), `frontend/src/__tests__/wayback.test.js` (new, 4 tests), `frontend/e2e/wayback.spec.js` (new, 3 tests), ADR-011 (new) status → Accepted, ADR-009 implementation note, roadmap batch 4 marked shipped, guide/README/CLAUDE pointers). Every node now has one exact, bounded timeline: **step 0 is its immutable born state; step N is immediately after its Nth chronicled interaction**. The selected mutation id is the cross-table cursor because second-precision timestamps cannot order same-second, non-commutative patches; material deltas still fold by their per-node monotonic version. The response reconstructs born properties + stored RFC 7396 deltas, derives causal pressure from chronicled strengths and art/sound wear from the same interaction prefix, and returns a deliberately actor-blind moment (`birth`, `trace`, `ripple`, or mechanical `change`) — no player, identity, or human/agent classification. First witness is the earliest existing row; the endpoint is canonical-world guarded, forged-node rejecting, read-rate-limited, and **provably read-only** (row count unchanged across GET). The roadmap's arrival sub-decision closes narrowly at its default: viewing Wayback creates no trace and no second arrival/page-view event; ADR-004's already-ratified participation ledger is unchanged. Both shipped clients expose the same scrub, first-witness line, manual birth→present range, and 850ms evolution animation (removed under reduced-motion); each reconstructed state feeds today's shared `nodeart.js`, and a deliberate listen gesture retunes today's shared `nodesound.js`. Cached fal.ai backgrounds are excluded because their current-history cache cannot honestly represent a historical frame. The surface says the contract aloud: **“the node as it was, seen with today's eyes”** — historical state, current presentation, reinterpretation rather than playback. Two implementation surprises were material. **(1) The sound engine keyed its active graph only by `(seed, node name)`, so scrubbing the same node would no-op even when its historical properties, pressure, and wear changed.** `soundscapeKey` now hashes a stable serialization of every sensory state input; equal states remain identical across property order, different states retune, and the initial enable stays inside the user's click gesture. **(2) Batch 1's SQLite overlay cache applied `json_patch` to the previous PATCH DOCUMENT. A null tombstone for a property supplied only by the born row therefore vanished (`json_patch('{}', '{"theme":null}') == '{}'`): Wayback correctly removed the property while the served present silently restored it.** The cache now folds born state + canonical deltas and derives one exact born-relative patch, because arbitrary RFC 7396 patch documents cannot be composed target-independently, and every hydration seam applies RFC 7396 rather than `dict.update`; the present-frame test proves born + chronicle fold = born + live overlay, including nested and born-key deletion. No history rewrite is needed. Additive migrations 0016–0017 store derived-cache metadata and pre-chronicle baselines beside the cache while `properties` remains a rollback-readable JSON object; hydration repairs tombstone drift from born rows and the chronicle, and cache-only legacy state survives its first chronicled write. Review hardening (PR #81 Copilot reviews: twenty-two inline findings plus one suppressed rate-budget note, all landed): the metadata-marked overlay cache materializes current state for O(1) hot writes and bulk hydration avoids per-node point reads; legacy baselines survive their first absolute and transition writes, lazy repair holds a write lock, rollback binaries keep reading plain-object caches, and an exact-blob plus chronicle-head marker catches even an unchanged tombstone write after re-upgrade; JSON booleans remain distinct from numbers, and the fold invariant begins at born state; only legacy or missing rows replay born state + canonical deltas once, including delete-an-object→add-a-child and upgrade repair regressions; React tracks an active archive preview so enabling sound cannot immediately retune it to the live node; both modals clear stale snapshots, coalesce scrub requests, carry dialog semantics, trap and restore focus, and close on Escape; the explorer also resets its play label when an autoplay fetch fails; the roadmap PR references, load-bearing file map, and test matrix are corrected. Verified: Ruff green; **903 Python passed** (was 891); **95 Vitest passed** (was 91); production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **11 Playwright E2E passed** under the production CSP (was 8), including present→birth scrub in both clients; both clients also inspected live in the in-app browser with zero console errors. **Irreversibility check: TRIPPED — awaiting owner confirmation before merge.** Additive migration 0016 creates one derived-cache metadata table and append-only 0017 adds its chronicle-head marker; they alter no pre-existing table or history, the prior binary ignores it and reads the unchanged plain-object cache, and re-upgrade validates both the blob and chronicle head. Continuity answer: deploy through the runbook path that takes a pre-deploy online backup; §8 requires a rehearsed restore on staging and a downloaded production backup before launch. No history rewrite, golden-world or puzzle-identity re-pin, new chronicle kind, new `world_mutations` row, or new write path. `/wayback` is read-only; the existing atomic substance writer retains future null tombstones in its rebuildable overlay cache but appends exactly the same immutable delta row it did before; era banks untouched.
- **The laws become learnable — the Causal Augury teaches the world's dynamics (pre-launch batch 3, ADR-010 ratified)** (`causality/forecast.py` (new), `puzzles/generators.py` (the family + hash election), `puzzles/types.py` (`PuzzleKind.PREDICTION`), `puzzles/quality.py` (classifier, world-reading set, raised floors), `tests/test_causal_prediction.py` (new, 14 tests), `tests/test_continuity_freeze.py` (conscious puzzle-identity re-pin, four digests), ADR-010 (new) status → Accepted, `docs/roadmap/pre-launch-window.md` batch 3 marked shipped, CLAUDE.md pointer, README puzzle row). The world-reading families teach structure; the Augury teaches DYNAMICS: a cry rises from the node and the player predicts how it carries under the sky's law — count its reach, name the living name of the scale where it last sounds, or find where Fractal skies ring it undimmed. **The answer is provably the engine's own behavior**: a pure `up_arm_forecast` restates the up-arm walk over the ancestor chain (per-hop dampening under the law of the landing scale, the 0.5 fallback above universes, Threadbare frays, Quantum tunnels, the MIN_STRENGTH floor), and the equivalence is pinned against the live bus across all eleven walkable laws on synthetic chains plus 200+ sampled launch-world nodes — zero mismatches. Election is a seed-pure hash BEFORE the weighted family draw (the lock branch's pattern): on seed 382, **430 of 4,077 eligible Region-and-deeper nodes elected (10.55%), 396 served at every renewal epoch (9.41% of all 4,208 puzzles)**, and the whole augury path runs in its own RNG domain, so all 34 structural declines (Inverted flip — the ancestor-chain purity contract can't see the children flip targets, and on the staged both-arm path flip is a no-op; Threadbare frays before the cry sounds; the rare node where every form leaks) fall through **byte-identically — measured: exactly the 396 served rows differ from a family-free build, 3,812 identical**, which is the whole blast radius of the re-pin. (The first cut consumed shared RNG before the leak screen and perturbed 6 extra nodes — caught by the byte-identity measurement, fixed with the dedicated domain.) Hints teach each law's temperament in authored fiction ("Fractal skies are self-similar: every second step keeps the cry's full voice"); gentle prompts name the law, hard prompts point at the universe whose property it is (and a terminus answer that IS the universe switches wording so the prompt never prints it — the standard leak screen backstops). The launch world's physics, measured from born rows: four universes carrying **Fractal (1,189 nodes), Threadbare (1,133), Tidal (786), Palindromic (1,099)** — question forms were chosen to be alive in this world (no Quantum on 382, so no tunnel form in v1; the forecast already computes tunneling for any future world that draws it). Ecology gate re-audit PASSES with floors raised (family_count 9→10, kind_count 7→8): world-reading **65.59%** (was 61.50%), decode **33.75%** (was 37.81%), largest family **19.94%** (keeper_witness; was 21.91%), prompts **99.90%** unique, answers **54.06%** unique, 11 families, 9 kinds. Difficulty covenant holds inside the family: the 396 span 94/102/108/92 across tiers 1–4, each node's own draw. Resolver-built and tree-built nodes serve identical Auguries (0 mismatches, the seal-gate contract); renewal epochs keep the family and rename. Review hardening (PR #80 owner review: one P1 + one P2, both landed): **(1) renewal epochs did not preserve family identity** — the content RNG folds in the epoch, so a renewal could pick a form whose answer leaks and fall through to an unrelated family (owner-measured: 394 epoch-0 Auguries became 393 at epoch 1 and 392 at epoch 2 — renewal silently changed a node's mechanics); the family now tries its remaining valid forms in deterministic order before falling through, and since form validity is epoch-independent, any node that can serve cleanly serves at EVERY epoch — measured post-fix: 396/396/396 at epochs 0/1/2, and the renewal test now covers every launch Augury at both pinned epochs instead of one sample (the fix also let two previously leak-rejected epoch-0 nodes serve, hence 394→396 and one further full-depth digest re-pin — the three depth-6 digests were already correct, all affected nodes lying below depth 6); **(2)** the class-scoped `launch_auguries` fixture was an instance method (`PytestRemovedIn10Warning` under pytest 9, a future failure under the unbounded `pytest>=8.0` pin) — moved to module scope, and the suite now runs warning-free. Verified (post-review): Ruff green; **891 Python passed** (was 877); **91 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **8 Playwright E2E passed** (`ENFOLDED_E2E=1`). **Irreversibility check:** TRIPPED, by design — this PR is the door ADR-010 ratifies, and the owner's merge enacts it (Status flipped to Accepted in this diff; the coverage, gate-class, Inverted, and re-pin decisions were each ratified in the owner's design interview before building). (1) **Intentional pre-launch puzzle-identity re-pin**: the four puzzle digests (full-depth, depth-6 epoch 0, renewal epochs 1–2) are consciously re-pinned per the `repin-goldens` procedure — production is unborn; already-born local/dev worlds keep their stored nodes but the 396 served nodes' prior generated puzzle solves do not carry; locked Rooms are untouched by construction (the lock branch short-circuits first), so seal continuity survives. No `GENERATOR_VERSION` bump (births unchanged — every name/property/world pin stayed green, the precedent of the 2026-08-04 puzzle re-pin). (2) No migration, no new `world_mutations` write path, no `world_meta` pin touched, era banks untouched.
- **Names shed their addresses on every player-facing surface — the display layer arrives** (`static/clientlogic.js` (`displayName`/`nodeAddress`), `multiverse/utils.py` (Python mirror), `frontend/src/names.js` (new), `frontend/src/{App.jsx,components/{TextPanel,SceneView}.jsx}`, `static/explorer.js`, `interface/__init__.py`, `frontend/src/__tests__/names.test.js` (new), display-contract updates in `tests/test_interface.py`, `frontend/src/__tests__/mutations.test.js`, and both E2E specs). An owner review of the batch-2 hinge surfaced the finding: "Hidden Thorn Quark-11431112111" *reads* like a random-string name even though the suffix is the node's deterministic path address, not entropy — the address was riding the display name and cluttering it. Resolution, owner-ratified: a display layer. Player-facing chrome shows the readable phrase ("Hidden Thorn Quark"); the address becomes its own field (a `⌖`-marked row in both browser sidebars, an `address` line in CLI `look`) and the full canonical name stays one gesture away (hover title on names in both browser clients). Coverage: the React scene label, side panel, passages, and travelers; the explorer's graph labels (with hover `<title>`), sidebar, and travelers; all ~24 event-feed narration lines across the shared `mutationLine` and both clients' inline feed strings; and the CLI's prompt, breadcrumb, map, look, passage list, seal line, and ambient-observation table. The rule is one canonical implementation (`clientlogic.js`), a strip-the-`-digits`-suffix mirror of the reading the puzzle layer already used (`puzzles/generators.py` `_living_name` — answers were always the phrase, so no gameplay surface moves). **What deliberately does NOT change:** every identity surface — chronicle rows, WS move payloads, position saves, art/sound keying, `resolve_node_by_name` — still speaks full canonical names, and server-generated fiction (puzzle prompts, node voices) is untouched because prompt text is pinned puzzle identity. Nine pre-existing tests that pinned the old suffixed display were updated to the new contract (the mid-scale "leaf node" line, breadcrumb/map/ambient renders, mutation narration, and four E2E exact-name assertions, which now also prove the address field and hover reveal). Review hardening (PR #79 owner review: one P2 coverage gap + one P3, both landed): **the first pass missed five live surfaces** — the React causal `EventRow`, App's "looking within" line, Interact's speak hint, and the explorer's observer table and sealed-line keeper all still rendered canonical names raw. Rather than patching each shell, the two live-narration builders moved into the shared layer (`clientlogic.js` `causalFeedLine`/`observationRow`, consumed by both clients) so the behavior is pinned once: three Vitest tests cover the causal feed and observer rows, and the explorer E2E now drives a real `/observe` stream and asserts the first row shows the phrase with the canonical name on hover. The P3: the cited puzzle-layer precedent was misnamed `_base_name` in three places — the function is `_living_name` (`puzzles/generators.py:541`); corrected. Verified (post-review): Ruff green; **877 Python passed** (was 875); **91 Vitest passed** (was 83); production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **8 Playwright E2E passed** (`ENFOLDED_E2E=1`). **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, no golden re-pin, no `GENERATOR_VERSION` change, no new `world_mutations` write path, no world_meta pin touched, era banks untouched; the diff is presentation-only (client chrome plus two pure helper functions), and every stored or transmitted name remains the canonical full form, so this is fully reversible.
- **The hierarchy closes into a loop — the wrap passage ships at the traversal layer (pre-launch batch 2, ADR-008 ratified)** (`multiverse/wrap.py` (new), `persistence/migrations/0015_world_meta.sql` (new), `persistence/__init__.py` (`get_world_meta`/`pin_world_meta`), `server/handlers.py` (/world wrap block, /speak hinge flag), `consciousness/__init__.py` (hinge lore), `interface/__init__.py`, `static/{clientlogic,explorer}.js`, `static/index.html`, `frontend/src/{App,components/TextPanel}.jsx`, `frontend/src/wrap.js` (new), `tests/test_wrap_passage.py` (new, 23 tests), `frontend/e2e/wrap.spec.js` (new), ADR-008 status → Accepted, `docs/roadmap/pre-launch-window.md` batch 2 marked shipped, CLAUDE.md one-way-door entry, `irreversibility-check` skill widened to world_meta pins). Descending below ANY SubatomicParticle now surfaces at the Multiverse root; ascending beyond the root lands at the world's ONE hinge particle. The hinge selector is a pure function of (seed, world as born) — it reads only stored `world_nodes` rows, so a fresh install of the same seed selects the same particle (pinned by test across two fresh DBs). The liveness census reproduced the owner-measured PR #76 numbers **exactly**: 1,505 particles on seed 382, 706 (46.9%) ineligible beneath a born-locked Room, 799 (53.1%) eligible. The tuned worthiness rule (born coherence, +1.0 for an entangled/recurring tendency, +0.5 for superposed spin, keyed-hash tie-break) lands on **Hidden Thorn Quark-11431112111** — entangled, superposed, coherence 0.988 (score 2.488; runner-up 2.464), its lineage's one Room born unlocked — a monument whose born character already speaks the loop's physics (its scale verb: "collapse a superposed spin by watching it"). At first ask the name is pinned write-once into the new `world_meta` table and from then on **the stored hinge is the hinge**: `pin_world_meta` refuses overwrite (8 concurrent first-askers converge on one value), persistence exposes no update/delete for the table, and `TestSelectorEditImmunity` — the wrap's mirror of `TestBankEditImmunity` — proves an edited selector cannot move a pinned monument. Transit runs the standard seal gate on every surface: the WS move path already gates every move (a root-side move to a particle under a locked Room stays `move_denied: sealed` — the loop is not a wormhole), and the CLI's two crossing paths consult `seal_check` explicitly (threshold-holds behavior pinned by monkeypatched-gate tests). Both browser clients share one affordance rule (`clientlogic.js wrapAffordance`/`firstWrapCrossing`, Vitest-covered): the /world response carries the wrap block (landings + authored lines) so every client speaks the same fiction; the React app and the D3 explorer each gained the two buttons, and crossing rides the existing jump machinery, which deepens the view when the hinge lies below the fetched horizon. The CLI mirrors this: an ascent from a depth-6 session rebuilds the session tree at full depth and lands on the hinge's true 11-node ancestry stack, so repeated ascent cycles the hinge's ancestor chain — the ADR's honest topology claim, observable in the terminal. The hinge's voice knows what it is (`consciousness.speak(hinge=True)` appends authored lore; `/speak` passes `wrap.is_hinge` — endpoint-verified), and first crossings speak an authored line once per session (CLI) / once per browser (localStorage, fail-open). Two batch surprises worth logging: **(1) the batch-1 `monkeypatch.undo()` trap struck again in this batch's own new test** — undoing the shared fixture reverted conftest's DB isolation and the escaped test pinned seeds 382 and 7001 into the real home DB mid-run (caught because the selector then answered from the wrong DB; cleaned up, and both affected tests now use dedicated `pytest.MonkeyPatch` instances exactly as batch 1's fix prescribed — the trap is clearly a pattern, not an incident); **(2) two `test_interface.py` tests pinned the old dead-end fiction** ("leaf node — no deeper paths" at a particle) and were updated to the new contract — mid-scale view-horizon leaves keep the dead-end line, particles now offer the passage. This batch also ran the Playwright suite remotely for the first time (batch 1's container lacked Chromium), which surfaced two harness finds: the pinned Playwright wanted browser build 1228 vs the container's 1194 (resolved with the config's existing `PW_CHROMIUM_PATH` override — no config change needed), and **(3) `check.sh`'s `ENFOLDED_E2E=1` branch had never actually executed** — it passed a repo-relative `ENFOLDED_PYTHON=.venv/bin/python`, which Playwright's webServer resolves from `frontend/` (exit 127; CI never hit it because CI installs into its runner python) — fixed to an absolute path in this diff. Review hardening (PR #78 owner review: two P2 findings, both landed): **(1) CLI crossings now enter the chronicle** — both `interface` wrap paths recorded nothing, so a `main.py play` crossing was absent from the permanent record and activity wear while the identical browser crossing left its `PLAYER_MOVE` row; each path now records the attributed move at its landing, only after the seal gate passes and the landing succeeds (a refused crossing leaves no trace — pinned by test); **(2) the standalone `main.py speak` path now passes the hinge flag** — it relied on the default `hinge=False`, so speaking directly to the hinge produced an ordinary-particle prompt; `cmd_speak` now passes `wrap.is_hinge`, with command-level coverage joining the endpoint test. Verified (post-review): Ruff green; **875 Python passed** (was 850); **83 Vitest passed** (was 77); production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **8 Playwright E2E passed** (`ENFOLDED_E2E=1`, both clients crossing both directions under the production CSP). **Irreversibility check:** TRIPPED, by design — this PR is the door ADR-008 ratifies, and the owner's merge enacts it (Status flipped to Accepted in this diff). (1) Additive migration `0015_world_meta.sql`: one new write-once table, no change to any existing table — continuity policy holds. (2) A new class of permanent world identity: the hinge pin, written once by `persistence.pin_world_meta` (no update or delete door exists; selector edits cannot move it). No production pin exists yet — the canonical world pins `Hidden Thorn Quark-11431112111` at first ask, and moving it afterward is an ADR-level continuity decision. No golden re-pin (generation untouched — `GENERATOR_VERSION`, banks, and freeze pins unchanged); no new `world_mutations` write path in kind — wrap transits chronicle as ordinary `PLAYER_MOVE` rows through the existing `record_mutation` recorder, though the review round added its two CLI call sites (owner-directed: the CLI's crossings had recorded nothing, unlike the browser's) — the chronicle stays append-only and its three maintenance mechanisms unchanged; era banks untouched.
- **The chronicle learns to remember state — every substance write chronicles its delta, atomically (pre-launch batch 1, ADR-009 ratified)** (`persistence/__init__.py`, `persistence/migrations/0014_chronicled_deltas.sql` (new), `causality/{__init__,wiring}.py`, `server/{handlers,heartbeat,world_mechanics}.py`, `interface/__init__.py`, `tests/test_chronicled_deltas.py` (new), ADR-009 status → Accepted, `docs/roadmap/pre-launch-window.md` batch 1 marked shipped). The one atomic write API lands: `persistence.record_substance_change` appends the chronicle row (RFC 7396 merge patch + triggering kind + strength), applies the same patch to the `node_runtime_state` overlay, and allocates the per-node monotonic `node_version` — one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, so a crash leaves neither half and concurrent writers serialize (measured: an 8-thread barrier on one node allocates versions 1–8 with zero errors; the immediate lock matters — a deferred transaction lets two writers read the same `MAX(node_version)` from the same WAL snapshot and die non-retryably on upgrade). `upsert_node_properties` is now module-internal (`_apply_overlay_patch`, callable only inside the transaction) — compliance is structural: there is no other door to the overlay. All six verified writers route through it: the causal effects handler (previously the full gap — neither delta nor strength chronicled), the scale-verb immediate branch on its three surfaces, the maturation drain, and constellation lighting (its `{"constellated": true}` delta now chronicled); entanglement resolution writes no properties and is untouched. **The batch's design surprise:** the wiring's record and effects handlers had to merge into one substance handler, because the ADR's two rules — "the delta rides the event's row" and "each fired event carries strength on exactly one row" — collide at the one place a producer-owned (`record=False`) origin also materially changes its node: a `PUZZLE_SOLVED` origin. Routing the effects handler naively would have written a second `PUZZLE_SOLVED` row, double-counting the ripple fold (0.2 vs the live 0.1 — the ripple-equals-fold test catches it), shadowing the attributed solver in `get_puzzle_solve`'s restart rehydration (the anonymous row sorts first), and inflating the `solves > rearms` renewal comparison. Resolution (as hardened in review — see below): producer-owned origins write their ONE attributed row through `causality.wiring.record_origin_event` / `record_verb_act`, which carry the origin strength (`causality.ORIGIN_STRENGTH`) AND the material delta on that same row, atomically with the overlay; `record=False` buses wire ripple only. `ripple_score` is formally a derived cache: `rebuild_ripple_scores` refolds chronicled strengths (min(1.0, Σ strength × 0.1), matching the live increment) and repairs drift (measured: a cache corrupted to 0.93 rebuilds to the fold's 0.1). Fold helpers ship for batch 4 to stand on (`json_merge_patch`, `fold_node_properties` behind the per-node version cursor; the wayback surface itself is deliberately not built). Migration 0014 is additive only: three nullable columns (`strength`, `delta`, `node_version`) plus one fold index; historical rows stay NULL. All four ADR test families land among 20 new behavior tests, including Python-fold-vs-SQLite-`json_patch` parity on nested merges and null-deletes at every cursor position. One harness surprise worth logging: pytest's function-scoped `monkeypatch` fixture is *shared* with conftest's DB isolation, so `monkeypatch.undo()` mid-test pointed persistence at the real home DB — the injected-failure test uses a dedicated `MonkeyPatch` instance instead. Review hardening (PR #77 owner review: three P1 blockers, all landed): **(1) transitions are computed under the serialization lock** — `apply_event_effects` derived deltas from a request-local snapshot *before* the transaction, so two concurrent `DANGER_ALERT`s from danger 5 both chronicled `{danger_level: 6}` (lost update, permanently mis-recorded); the new `record_substance_transition` API reads the live overlay under `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` and calls a pure compute (`multiverse/effects.compute_event_effects` split out; verb effects re-derived the same way via `record_verb_act`, since `_ward`/`_inscribe`/`_seed` are the same read-modify-write shape) — measured: 4 concurrent danger alerts now land 6→7→8→9 and 3 concurrent inscriptions land 1→2→3, versions distinct, fold equal to overlay; **(2) the pre-review `EVENT_EFFECT` two-row shape is gone** — it left the attributed `PUZZLE_SOLVED` commit and its delta in separate transactions, so a crash between them made a solve canonical while its delta was lost forever (restart rehydrates solved, nothing replays); the producer-owned origin now lands row + delta + overlay in ONE transaction (`record_origin_event`), and the injected-failure family covers the producer window (a crash leaves no solve row at all — fail closed); **(3) the migration runner is atomic per file** — `executescript` autocommits each statement, so an interruption mid-file stranded committed `ALTER`s without their `schema_version` marker and the retry died on `duplicate column name` (latent since 0009, promoted to P1 by this batch's permanent-path migration); `_run_migrations` now executes a file's statements and its version marker in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, with an interrupted-migration recovery test. Verified (post-review): Ruff green; **850 Python passed** (was 830); **77 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green. **Irreversibility check:** TRIPPED, by design — this PR is the door ADR-009 ratifies, and the owner's merge enacts it (Status flipped to Accepted in this diff). (1) Additive migration `0014_chronicled_deltas.sql`: three nullable columns + one index on `world_mutations`, no destructive rewrite, continuity policy holds. (2) A consciously widened chronicle write surface: `record_substance_change` is a new `world_mutations` INSERT (the one atomic substance API) and the substance handler adds `EVENT_EFFECT` rows — the chronicle stays append-only; redaction, double-gated pruning, and disaster restore remain the only maintenance mechanisms, and deltas are mechanical fields that survive redaction. No golden re-pin (generation untouched — `GENERATOR_VERSION`, banks, and freeze pins unchanged); era banks untouched.
- **The pre-launch window becomes a decided plan — the hierarchy will close into a loop, and the chronicle learns to remember state, not just events** (`docs/decisions/ADR-008-wrap-passage.md` (new), `docs/decisions/ADR-009-chronicled-deltas.md` (new), `docs/roadmap/pre-launch-window.md` (new), `docs/evaluation/2026-08-10-recursion-and-time.md` (new), `CLAUDE.md` pointers). An owner design thread (recursion, strange loops, observer-driven growth, dimensionality, time) is synthesized into decisions before anything is deployed — the window in which they are cheap, and in one case the only window in which the decision is possible at all. **ADR-008 (wrap passage):** the eleven scales close into a loop at the traversal layer — every particle descends to the Multiverse root (every part enfolds the whole), the root ascends to one seed-chosen hinge particle (a discoverable monument in the shared world). Traversal-layer only because four load-bearing surfaces walk `node.parent` expecting a finite chain (`law_for`, `sealing_room`, the lineage puzzle families, `__repr__`); causality does not wrap in v1 (Fractal/Recursive law profiles carry full-strength hops — no convergence guarantee, and the staged queue would never drain); wrap transit runs the standard seal gate so the hinge can't become a wormhole past a lock. **ADR-009 (chronicled deltas):** the batch's verified surprise — node-state-at-T is *unreconstructible* today, three pieces missing: mutation rows store `event.payload` without `strength` (`causality/wiring.py:41`), the effects handler persists property deltas to the overlay without chronicling them, and the overlay is a `json_patch` current-state merge with no history (`persistence/__init__.py:805`). Replay is no fix (needs the unrecorded strength; ties history to current effects code — the era-names lesson at world scale). The decision: every substance write chronicles its delta + strength; state-at-T := born row + fold of deltas; a fold-equals-overlay invariant test enforces the discipline. Since an archive is only as complete as its earliest recording, this is **batch 1 of the pre-launch sequence and must precede any production history**. The roadmap sequences four batches (chronicled deltas → wrap passage → causal-prediction puzzle family through the ecology gate → the wayback surface, which renders reconstructed past state through the *current* deterministic art/sound functions — reinterpretation, not playback; stored nowhere, identical for every player at a given deploy) and records what was declined so future sessions don't relitigate: a second dimensional scale (its payoff is already mechanically real in the 12 per-Universe law profiles; every scale is a full content vertical), wrapped causality v1, and extensive frontier growth (the stance is intensive infinity — finite extent closed by the loop, unbounded deepening under attention; if ADR-006's evolution trigger ever fires, growth is breadth-only, since ADR-008 forecloses depth 12: below the particle is the whole). Both ADRs are Proposed — drafted from the thread's recommendations, ratification lands at the implementing PRs' merge gates. Review hardening (PR #76 owner review: two blockers + seven findings, all folded in): ADR-009 now specifies **one atomic write API** — a single transaction chronicles the delta, applies the overlay merge patch, and allocates a per-node monotonic version, with injected-failure and concurrent-writer tests joining fold-equals-overlay (which detects divergence but cannot prevent it — atomicity is the guarantee, the test is the net); state-at-T folds in per-node version order, since second-precision `recorded_at` cannot order non-commutative patches sharing a timestamp; the writer inventory is corrected to the six verified call sites — entanglement resolution writes no properties, while the CLI/heartbeat verb branches (`interface/__init__.py:213`, `server/heartbeat.py:194`) and the maturation drain (`server/heartbeat.py:409`) do, and constellation lighting chronicles without its delta; ADR-008's hinge is **pinned immutably at first selection** (permanent world identity must not depend on mutable selector code) and constrained to an unsealed lineage as a liveness invariant — owner-measured: **706 of 1,505 particles (46.9%)** sit beneath a locked Room on seed 382, near coin-flip odds of shipping a half-dead loop unconstrained; the loop's topology claim is corrected (consistent descent returns every traveler to the *whole*, not to their origin — the fiction must not promise origin-return); wayback is described honestly as past state through present renderers (state is historical, rendering presentational; renderer versioning is a named revisit trigger); and the roadmap now marks only batch 1 as architecturally forced — batches 2–4 are an explicit product-scope choice — with its decisions table re-titled *proposed*, ratification at each implementing PR's merge gate. A second review round closed the last consistency gaps: the persisted `ripple_score` is declared a **derived, rebuildable cache** outside the atomic transaction (its live increment is monotonic, capped at 1.0, decay-free — verified sole production caller `causality/wiring.py:49` — so it folds purely from chronicled strengths; a rebuild function and ripple-equals-fold invariant join the batch-1 tests, and producer-attributed `record=False` rows must carry strength since they are their event's only trace); ADR-008's "no new rows" claim is narrowed to no-`world_nodes`-change — the hinge pin IS one additive first-selection metadata record, so batch 2 may carry an additive migration; batch 1's dependency claim is limited to historical completeness and wayback (batches 2–3 don't need deltas); and the wayback reinterpretation semantics now read the same in the ADR Context, the roadmap, and this entry. Verified (docs-only diff; full suite re-run regardless): Ruff green; **830 Python passed**; **77 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green. **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, no golden re-pin, no new `world_mutations` write path, era banks untouched; decision records, a roadmap doc, an evaluation synthesis, and CLAUDE.md pointers only, all markdown. ADR-009 *ratifies* a future widening of the chronicle write surface, but that door is walked through — with its own tripped check — in the implementing batch-1 PR, not here.
- **The working rules gain a harness layer — remote sessions bootstrap themselves and the merge rituals become invocable** (`.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/hooks/session-start.sh`, `.claude/skills/{irreversibility-check,changelog-entry,repin-goldens}/SKILL.md`, `.github/pull_request_template.md`, `.gitignore`, `CLAUDE.md`). The repo audit's verdict: the agentic baseline is strongest exactly where it is enforced by code and weakest where it rides each cold session's reading comprehension — so three working rules move from prose into the harness. **Bootstrap:** a SessionStart hook provisions Claude Code on the web onto the pinned toolchain before the first turn. The remote container's default `node` is v22.x, which `setup.sh` correctly refuses (`.nvmrc` pins 20.19+); the hook selects the container's `/opt/node20` (v20.20.2), persists it into the session PATH via `CLAUDE_ENV_FILE`, then runs the canonical `./setup.sh` behind a locked-inputs stamp (`pyproject.toml`, both Python locks, `frontend/package-lock.json`, the version pins, `setup.sh` itself). Measured: first run **40.4 s** (full venv + `npm ci`), healthy re-run **0.62 s** warm (≈5 s cold-cache), so resume/clear/compact restarts never re-pay the `npm ci` rebuild; local sessions are untouched (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` guard). **Fewer prompts:** project settings pre-allow only the canonical verification commands — `check.sh`, `setup.sh`, Ruff, pytest, the frontend test/build pair, and the wheel smoke — as exact command forms. **Rituals as skills:** `irreversibility-check` walks the diff over the four one-way-door surfaces (freeze pins / banks / `GENERATOR_VERSION`, `persistence/migrations/`, `record_mutation` call sites, the era display banks) and emits the required 2–3 line check; `changelog-entry` pins this file's house format (headline + files, measured evidence, `Verified:` counts, closing check); `repin-goldens` encodes the conscious re-pin procedure (ratify first, bump `GENERATOR_VERSION`, both depths plus renewal epochs, era banks never). A PR template gives the check a named slot in every PR body, and CLAUDE.md now points at the layer with the sync rule that a skill contradicting it is drift. Review hardening (PR #75, four findings, all landed): the allowlist drops its prefix wildcards and git rules entirely — a `:*` rule would have auto-approved mutating forms like `ruff check --fix` or `git diff --output=<tracked file>`; the hook command quotes `$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` so a checkout path containing a space resolves (the unquoted form measured failing, exit 127); the skip path probes the toolchain instead of trusting directory existence — `ruff --version`, `pytest --version`, `npm ls --all`, where **default `npm ls` passed with a deleted transitive package** and `--all` catches it at the same ~0.4 s (a deleted `chai` was detected and repaired end-to-end), with the stamp dropped before reinstall so an interrupted run cannot leave a matching stamp behind; and the irreversibility-check skill diffs against the fetched PR base instead of a possibly stale local `main`. Verified: hook executed end-to-end twice in this remote container (timings above); then, inside the hook-provisioned environment: Ruff green; **830 Python passed**; **77 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green (the post-review diff touches only shell/JSON/markdown harness files; Ruff re-run green). **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, no golden re-pin, no new `world_mutations` write path; harness configuration, a PR template, and documentation only.
- **The world becomes the puzzle, and the pitch evidence now comes from the launch world** (`puzzles/{generators,quality}.py`, `scripts/{puzzle_quality,capture_pitch_assets,pitch_capture_server}.*`, `tests/test_puzzle_quality.py`, pitch assets/brief, launch evaluations/roadmap). The full seed-382 audit confirmed that prompt uniqueness had hidden mechanic repetition: anagram + Caesar cipher + numeric pattern served **2,974 / 4,208 nodes (70.68%)**. Two resolver-safe families make the materialized world itself the answer source: **Keeper Witness** uses readable ancestor names as landmarks; **Ancestral Compass** combines edge letters from immutable properties at two enclosing scales. Names deliberately remain visible for orientation: 1–2★ Keepers ask for one landmark, while 3–4★ Keepers compose a new phrase across two or three ancestors so the answer cannot be copied whole from the tree. Contextual locked Rooms remain authoritative. The executable launch gate now caps decode families and any single family while flooring world-reading share, prompt/answer uniqueness, and family/kind coverage. Measured after review hardening: decode **37.81%**, world-reading **61.50%**, largest family **21.91%**, unique prompts **99.86%**, unique answers **54.42%**, ten mechanic families and all eight kinds. The multi-word no-leak invariant is mechanical, renewal suffixes classify correctly, unknown generator families fail the census loudly, and the test module builds the 4,208-node census once instead of roughly seven times (focused puzzle suite: **~1s**). The stale generator-v1/seed-42 pitch images are replaced by live generator-v2/seed-382 captures: Archivist drops at readable Planet `Golden Pilgrim Eden-12321`; a real 3★ composite Keeper solve cascades from `×1.00` to `×0.13`; the first-child chain displays all eleven art families; Tessera moves live as a tender. A reproducible harness uses an ephemeral loopback port, verifies the served seed and manifest, drives the real server/browser against an isolated temporary DB, cleans up without a lingering timer, and stores machine-readable provenance beside the assets. Verified: Ruff green; puzzle gate PASS; **830 Python passed**; **77 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; capture completed and all four assets visually inspected. **Irreversibility check: intentional pre-launch puzzle-identity re-pin** — production is undeployed/unborn; depth-6, full-world, renewal-1, and renewal-2 puzzle digests change and are consciously re-pinned before first production birth. Already-born local/dev worlds keep their stored nodes but their prior generated puzzle solves do not carry to the new identities. No migration, stored-node rewrite, generator-version change, or new `world_mutations` write path.
- **The launch world is now curated rather than merely configured** (`multiverse/{generator,quality,store}.py`, `scripts/world_quality.py`, both browser clients, launch config/runbook/ADR-007, golden pins). The pre-launch audit confirmed the owner's concern: roughly 79% of sampled v1 base names were invented one-word syllable strings, and the path suffix hid repeated display names instead of making them memorable. Generator v2 replaces that surface with collision-free three-word semantic names allocated injectively from curated English banks (6,912 phrases per level, wider than the 6,144-node theoretical maximum); name/property/breadth determinism is now domain-separated. An executable player-facing census gates readability, exact base-name uniqueness, aspect/property uniqueness, categorical coverage, sibling distinction, branching variation, level reachability, and size, then ranked seeds 1–512 without touching the store: 510 passed; **seed 382 ranked first** (99.5973, 4,208 nodes) over provisional 42 (97.4487). Full soundscape inspection found 4,208/4,208 distinct parameter fingerprints and 1,524/1,524 sonically distinct sibling pairs. Both clients now turn a depth-window horizon into **Look within**, fetching one more prefix level while keeping the player at the same stored node; traveler jumps deepen to their target, so Rooms through particles are no longer hidden behind an advanced control. Full verification also caught a true upgraded/legacy duplicate: the old static “four-digit lock” could still impersonate the contextual keeper-key LOCK. It is retired, and every `locked` Room now serves its parent-Region truth as the authoritative puzzle at every renewal epoch. Launch, CLI, generator, store, heartbeat fallback, and census defaults change 42 → 382 so an omitted local seed cannot accidentally birth the retired provisional world. `GENERATOR_VERSION` deliberately changes 1 → 2; depth-6/full-world name/property/puzzle pins and renewal-epoch puzzle pins are consciously re-pinned to launch seed 382. Review hardening restores saved depth across devices in both clients, keeps the explorer's history feed and WebSocket session stable while deepening, reloads the causal pump's canonical seed each tick, asserts the launch seed's quality gate in CI, and replaces redundant source greps with browser behavior. The census records remaining systemic decode-family dominance rather than claiming seed selection fixes it. Verified: Ruff green; **823 Python passed**; **77 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; Playwright **6/6**, including horizon crossing, session stability, and deep cross-device resume in both clients. **Irreversibility check: intentional pre-launch birth change** — no production world exists; already-born local worlds remain immutable; after production birth, seed/name changes require the ADR-007 continuity process.
- **One shared canonical launch world — ADR-007 ratified and enforced** (`docs/decisions/ADR-007-one-shared-launch-world.md`, `server/{guard,handlers,websocket,heartbeat}.py`, `causality/staging.py`, persistence queue claims, both browser clients, Fly/env config, behavior/deploy/E2E tests). The project owner made the interaction premise explicit: humans and agents must inherit and alter one communal place; player-created seed worlds would fragment presence, memory, puzzles, causality, and the chronicle. `NESTED_WORLDS_CANONICAL_SEED` is now the operator-owned launch boundary (the immediately following curation pass selected committed seed 382; an explicit blank/off value preserves local test/dev multi-world work). Every public seed path now shares one resolver: missing selects canonical, matching remains compatible, mismatch returns 400 **before a node lookup or lazy birth**; `/worlds`, stale position restore, WebSocket rooms, the autonomous heartbeat, and background causal/maturation queue drains are confined too (old dev-world work stays durable but paused). Both clients have retired seed creation/switching (and the explorer's already-ignored breadth inputs); view depth remains only a prefix presentation of the same stored world, and clients learn internal identity from `/world`. The CLI retains `--seed` for operator curation and isolated generation analysis. New behavior tests pin REST read/write refusal, no-birth-on-refusal, WebSocket refusal, heartbeat/queue confinement, fail-closed default, and deployment configuration. Verified: Ruff green; **814 Python passed**; **74 Vitest passed**; production build fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; Playwright **3/3**. **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, golden birth re-pin, deletion, or new `world_mutations` path. This restricts access to existing alternate seeds without rewriting or removing their stored data; changing the canonical seed after launch is explicitly a future ADR-level continuity decision.
- **PR #72 review follow-up pins the package seams the first hardening pass introduced** (`pyproject.toml`, `scripts/verify_wheel.py`, `server/websocket.py`, `requirements.lock`, `tests/{test_frontend_contract,test_moderation}.py`; roadmap 4.9). Static package data now uses recursive `app/**/*` / `easter-egg/**/*` globs, and the installed-wheel verifier requires the shared client rules, `/app` entry point, and at least one content-hashed frontend asset instead of proving only the explorer files. Three source-substring assertions called out by the review as the same grep-test pattern behind two earlier P0s are removed; the real load-order contract stays pinned while Vitest continues executing the shared behavior. The wheel smoke docstring now says its ambient dependencies are intentional, the WebSocket extraction documents the handler surface it consumes, and `setuptools` is marked as the build backend required by Docker's no-isolation install. Hashed, generated, runtime-layered Python locks remain a deliberate follow-up at the next dependency bump (roadmap 4.9), rather than being smuggled into this review pass. Full-suite verification exposed an unrelated deterministic test race: HTTP 101 could return before the second WebSocket player joined its room, so the moderation broadcast test now waits for each existing `welcome` frame (two consecutive timeouts before the fix; targeted pass afterward). Verified: Ruff green; **805 Python passed**; **74 Vitest passed**; recursive wheel build/install smoke green. **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, golden-world re-pin, or new `world_mutations` write path; packaging declarations, tests, and seam documentation only.
- **The agentic-coding baseline is reproducible, distributable, and less duplicated** (`AGENTS.md`, `.python-version`, `.nvmrc`, `requirements{,-dev}.lock`, `setup.sh`, `scripts/{check.sh,verify_wheel.py}`, `pyproject.toml`, CI/Docker, `static/clientlogic.js`, `server/{websocket,world_mechanics}.py`). The repo audit's implementation batch landed in dependency order. **Toolchain and bootstrap:** Python 3.11, Node 20.19+, setuptools, Ruff, and the complete runtime/dev dependency graphs are pinned; `setup.sh` now rejects wrong runtimes and installs a real `.venv` plus `npm ci` instead of announcing readiness after installing only `uv`; Ruff's intended E4/E7/E9/F gate is explicit, so a future default-rule change cannot silently redefine CI; `scripts/check.sh` is the one-command lint/test/build/bundle/wheel path, with opt-in real-browser E2E. The Vite/Vitest line moved to 8.2.0/4.1.10 after the old lock reported 7 advisories (1 critical, 2 high); the new lock reports **0 vulnerabilities**, all JS behavior tests remain green, and the main production chunk fell ~530 KB → 428 KB. **Distribution:** wheels now carry `main.py`, all migrations, the complete browser UI, and an `enfolded` CLI; CI builds, installs, initializes a fresh migrated DB, resolves packaged static assets, and runs CLI help from outside the checkout. Docker no longer masks an expected failed editable install with `|| true`; it installs the locked runtime and the real wheel-shaped project, then launches the installed CLI. **Discovery and duplication:** `AGENTS.md` points every agent to the one authoritative `CLAUDE.md`; the obsolete generator-side node resolver is removed (the stored-world resolver is now the sole runtime/test contract); the unused first-cut `ambienceParams`/`freq` sound API is retired; entry selection, passage badges, and chronicle narration moved from hand-mirrored client copies into `static/clientlogic.js`, used by both `/` and `/app`. **Boundaries:** the entire WebSocket lifecycle moved out of `handlers.py` into `server/websocket.py`, while constellations, entanglement, and canonical node hydration moved into `server/world_mechanics.py` (dispatch file 1,740 → 1,409 lines; routing/co-op extraction remains explicitly scheduled). Documentation now surfaces the composed per-node soundscape and the current model/test counts; `.DS_Store` noise is ignored and removed. Verified: Ruff green; **805 Python passed**; **74 Vitest passed**; Vite production build + committed bundle fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; Playwright **3/3** against the real Python server under production CSP (including the explorer's shared rules and WebAudio graph); npm audit 0. **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration added or altered, no golden world re-pin, and no new `world_mutations` write path; packaging, tooling, code ownership, and equivalent client/server behavior only.
- **The senses batch — the default surface now leads with the game's best material** (ensemble action plan Track 2, items 2.1–2.4; `static/index.html`, `static/explorer.js`, `static/guide.html`, `docs/pitch/beta-brief.md` + `docs/pitch/assets/` (new), `frontend/e2e/smoke.spec.js`). The ensemble's convergent finding was that the infrastructure had outrun the presentation — the composed art, the soundscape, and the pitch's visual proof were all present and all elsewhere. Four fixes, all behavior-verified: **(1) The sigil is the hero** — the explorer sidebar widens 300→340px and the node's generative art grows 268×140→308×232, leading the location panel instead of thumbnailing beside it. **(2) The sound invitation** — the soundscape was the game's least-discoverable rich surface (off by default behind a small toggle, zero mentions in the guide); the world now offers once per session, in fiction ("*The world hums, softly, in every place. Listen?*"), three seconds after the world settles — the click IS the WebAudio activation gesture browsers require; declining leaves only the header toggle; `sessionStorage`-scoped so it never nags. `/guide` gains a "The world hums — listen" section (was: zero occurrences of sound/audio). **(3) The engine room is tucked away** — the header's Seed/Depth/Breadth/Generate controls (the 2026-07-04 audit's "admin console wearing a theme") hide behind a ⚙ affordance (`aria-expanded`); `jumpTo`'s view-deepening still drives the hidden inputs unchanged. **(4) The four pitch captures are real** — `docs/pitch/assets/{dropin.png, cascade.gif, artgrid.png, heartbeat.png}` captured live from seed 42 in headless Chromium and verified by inspection: the drop-in shows the hero sigil expressing the node's own properties (rust-red sky bands, jungle horizon, four moons); the 22s cascade GIF (204 KB) shows a Region solve's consequence arriving ring by ring **with dampening legible in the feed numbers (+0.50 Planet, +0.25 System)**; the art grid shows all 11 form families distinct, labeled, drawn by the real module; the heartbeat still catches Tessera and Petrichor abroad unattended, banter tic included. The brief's Visuals section replaces its four PLACEHOLDER slots with the real captures and drops "no image assets on purpose" for "every image is a capture, never an authored asset". E2E grew: the explorer smoke now proves the engine room hidden-then-⚑-openable and drives the sound invitation as the activation path (accept → full WebAudio graph builds → toggle off). Suites: e2e 3/3, Vitest 77 unchanged, Python 800 unchanged (static-file-only client changes; `/app` bundle untouched). **Irreversibility check:** none — no migration, no golden re-pin, no new `world_mutations` write path; presentation and docs only.
- **The world is data now — ADR-006 Option A ratified and shipped: the freeze is retired, the world is materialized** (`multiverse/store.py` (new), `persistence/` + additive migration 0013 `world_nodes`, read-path swaps in `server/handlers.py` / `server/heartbeat.py` / `causality/staging.py` / `main.py` / `interface/`, `tests/test_world_store.py` (new, 10 behavior tests), covenant re-scopes in `CLAUDE.md` + the freeze-suite docstring, ADR-006 → Accepted). The project owner challenged the freeze-at-launch stance — *the goal is an evolving world; is the freeze truly necessary for memory?* — and ratified **Option A: materialize before launch**. The runtime no longer regenerates the world from the content banks per request; the generator runs **once per seed as a birthing tool** (lazy, on first visit), born rows become the node's identity, and every read path — `/world`, node resolution, WS root/resume/move, heartbeat, staged-cascade drain, CLI world/agent/puzzles/speak, terminal play — serves the stored world. Births are idempotent and race-safe (`save_world_nodes` refuses to overwrite; a lost birth race defers to the winner): **a born world is never re-born**. The covenant that replaces the freeze is *executed, not prose*: `TestBankEditImmunity` prepends a syllable that would rename essentially every node under regeneration and proves a born world serves identical trees and resolutions, while a world born after the edit expresses it — **banks govern births, and nothing else**. Store ≡ generator equivalence is pinned at both depths (names, levels, properties, child order) plus full resolution parity including every forgery refusal. Measured: birth = 4,439 rows in ~350 ms once per seed ever; serving depth 6 ~8 ms and full depth ~82 ms vs ~7/~109 ms under the old per-request regeneration — the pivot is performance-neutral-to-better. The golden pins survive re-scoped (they now describe what generator v1 *births* — fresh installs and future seeds — with `GENERATOR_VERSION` in `multiverse/store.py` as the conscious-change lever); **era names remain the one read-time generative surface** (their two display banks in `multiverse/chronicle.py` stay frozen and pinned until eras are materialized — recorded in ADR-006 "Revisit when"). What is deliberately NOT built yet: evolution mechanics themselves (frontier growth, new puzzle families via renewal, chronicled `WORLD_EVOLVED` change) — the store makes them possible; each waits on the evolution-event grammar and stays a one-way door under the merge gate. Suites: 790 → **800 passed** (+10 store tests; full suite green on the first post-pivot run), E2E 3/3 in real Chromium, CLI birth-then-reload verified on a fresh seed. **Irreversibility check: this diff IS a one-way door, ratified in writing** — additive migration 0013; the read-path swap makes the DB the sole authority for world content (backups now protect the world itself, not just its history — Litestream's priority rises); no golden re-pin (pins unchanged, docstring re-scoped); no new `world_mutations` write path.

Completion distinguishes advice, drafts, implementation, verification, and authorized publication; the CHANGELOG skill places batches under `[Unreleased]` and records only checks run. World covenants remain verbatim; birth identity remains immutable while existing chronicled state changes are described accurately. Canonical verification, specific owner approval for unchanged pin scope, consequential human decisions, PR-specific merge authorization, and the auto-merge prohibition remain. **Verified:** three skill structures valid; skill names and explicit invocation/permission settings unchanged; hook/check configuration unchanged; local references resolve; historical CHANGELOG preserved; ten-file documentation diff reviewed; fifteen written trigger/completion scenarios checked; diff whitespace check passes. Application suites were not run locally for this documentation-only draft; the canonical pre-merge gate remains required. Discovery memory survives while recent material changes and puzzle renewal create separate, durable action opportunities. A recent-change lane screens at most 64 candidates for current eligibility before assigning two slots; a fair cursor inspects at most 40 traversal candidates, visits at most 10, and admits at most four puzzle/persona attempts per tick; own/cross-cast effects cannot sustain a repair/decay loop. Quiet ticks retain context. Constructed 4,208-node saturation across 12 cast ticks produced **0 → 6 persona acts**, with **zero fresh discoveries** in both versions; a four-visit fixture previously made **80 recursive calls**, now at most 16 inspections. A real solve/decay renewal changes the agent from the stale puzzle to the current epoch without changing difficulty. Markers commit with existing M1/M2 acceptance; three SIGKILL phases, concurrent ticks, pending backup/restore, v19 upgrade, source-attributed delivery/reload and both clients preserve recovery, human puzzle boundaries and response ownership. M3’s #96 merge is recorded without implying deployment. Review follow-up fixes observer drop-ins below dangerous ancestors, consumed/ineligible priority entries, capacity-exhausted revisits, unused home-affinity draws, duplicate seal structure, malformed past-tense context and zero-count completion notices. Initial home affinity seeds the fair cursor; later ticks resume it. Eighteen additional regressions and both clients cover these findings. The full priority pool measures **64 screens + 40 traversal inspections, 30 reads and one hydration**. The saturated cast still makes six persona acts with **16 chronicle rows** before drains (initial M4: 20). Verified: Ruff green; **1,080 Python passed** (135.75s); **110 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **35 Playwright passed** (2.2m), including two new saturated-inhabitant cases. **Irreversibility check:** additive migration 0020 stores only attention markers, a scan cursor and a partial read index; attention shares existing acceptance transactions. Existing chronicle producers are bounded/staged, with no new event kind, historical rewrite, golden re-pin, born-node, hinge, era or causal-law edit.

The disposable HTTP/CLI/cast probe previously narrated **15 arriving ripples** as new local acts; those rows now identify the recorded source label, canonical location and source action number. Four delayed outcomes retain their authored flavor while resolving to four distinct origins. **All 24 raw history rows match the M2 baseline exactly**, excluding timestamps. Bounded pages batch source/delivery lookups; production pump notices project once per seed per partial batch (eight commits or a soft 50ms budget checked between deliveries), and both clients recover through authoritative reload without extra world/history reads per notice. Review fixes remove narration from 1,000/50-row image/banter reads (one SELECT, no projection), correct first-hand trace wording, reject malformed legacy waits/patches, and share the payload guard. Legacy gaps remain explicit; no actor taxonomy or inferred identity is added. **Verified:** `ENFOLDED_E2E=1 ./scripts/check.sh` — Ruff green, **1,034 Python passed** (38 new M3 cases plus retained M1/M2 coverage), **110 Vitest passed**, production bundle byte-fresh, installed-wheel smoke passed, **33 Playwright passed** (four new M3 cases plus all 29 existing cases). Two real HTTP kindles at density 418 previously landed absolute 438 twice; new v2 work lands 438 then 459. Repeated mark-only requests at density 999 now share one work ID and one origin/landing instead of two. Acceptance pins adjustment/flag components and a frozen interpreter; maturity respects live state, records actual deltas or explicit terminal no-ops, and retains M1's atomic delivery and completed fences. V1 patches and causal semantics remain unchanged, including distinct identical legacy deltas. Both clients expose authoritative pending state and explain terminal no-ops after missed notifications/reload. Review fixes preserve navigation and personal responses across delayed replies and other players' broadcasts, restore wait estimates, combine mixed-version public counts without merging work, and use bounded node reads with HTTP/socket-echo deduplication. Immediate saturated HTTP/cast attempts avoid writer reservations, while intended changes recheck under the lock. No current delayed action is exclusive; that future M0/M5 commitment proposal and ADR-013 remain unratified. No golden re-pin, born-node/hinge/era change or history rewrite. Disposable pending-work backup/restore covers the launch runbook §8 restore scenario; deployment and merge remain unapproved.


- **Accepted consequences survive interrupted delivery (M1)** (`persistence/` and additive migration 0018, `causality/{delivery,staging,wiring}.py`, HTTP/CLI/heartbeat producers, queue regressions and both browser clients). Both old delete-on-claim queues lost **1 of 1** accepted items after SIGKILL, leaving **0 history rows and 0 recovered work**; the first **4** application/initial-scheduling regressions also failed on the baseline. Acceptance now commits its origin and all initial work together; each delivery rechecks its durable ID under SQLite's writer lock and commits effects, deltas, pressure/rearm, continuations and retained completion together. Failed input stays pending with inspectable error/backoff; intentional stops and missing nodes have explicit outcomes. **47 recovery regressions** cover real process death, fresh-interpreter restart, competing workers, duplicate candidates, application/continuation/commit failures, malformed/future-version inputs, request paths and pending-work migration/backup/restore. Two browser tests kill/restart the server after a real Kindle and recover through authoritative reload; the explorer's response path now waits for maturation before projecting the patch. Review regressions also cover failed retry-metadata writes, room-owned cache rollback and read-only solved attempts, and sub-second queued `matures_in: 0` versus immediate `null`. Causal hydration now runs once per seed outside the writer lock, with live node/ancestor overlays inside it; three disposable 32-hop trials reduced median time **0.7992s → 0.0580s** and tree calls under the lock **32 → 0**. Legacy absolute patches and causal physics remain unchanged; M2 semantics are deferred. Completed rows remain indefinitely, and old/new workers must not be mixed. The [evidence](evaluation/2026-09-08-m1-delivery.md), [proposed ADR-019](decisions/ADR-019-m1-sqlite-delivery.md), and [operations guide](infrastructure/delivery-recovery.md) specify reviewable contracts and rollback limits; ADR-013 remains Proposed. Verified: Ruff green; **955 Python passed** in 88.66s; **95 Vitest passed**; production bundle byte-fresh; installed-wheel smoke green; **18 Playwright passed**.

  No golden re-pin, new event kind, born-node/hinge/era change, historical rewrite, or destructive migration. Pending-work migration and backup/restore are rehearsed on disposable databases; mixed old/new workers and in-place binary downgrade are unsupported.
  These persistent choices remain proposed for development-team review. No deployment, merge, or auto-merge is included.

- **`/register` was CSP-dead — the self-service invite flow's page logic never ran in a CSP-enforcing browser** (`static/register.js` (new), `static/register.html`, `server/handlers.py`, `frontend/e2e/smoke.spec.js`, `tests/test_day_one_recording.py`; ensemble-evaluation finding, action-plan item 1.1). The page shipped its entire logic — invite-param read, panel swap, submit handler — as an inline `<script>` under the server's `Content-Security-Policy: script-src 'self'` (no `unsafe-inline`, no nonce), so browsers blocked it wholesale: a self-service invitee saw a form that silently did nothing (the CSP-blocked-PixiJS failure class, recurring on the one page the E2E suite didn't drive). The logic now lives in external `static/register.js`, served ungated beside its page (`/register.js` route + gate-exemption list); the page references it with `defer` and carries no inline script. Verified two ways: a new Playwright smoke test drives `/register` in real Chromium under the production CSP — both assertions (no-invite panel swap; submit → server 403 rendered as the player-facing error line) only pass if the script executed — and a server behavior test pins the route, content type, CSP header, and no-inline-script contract. E2E suite 2 → 3 tests, all green against the real server (the bogus token's expected `Failed to load resource` console line is allow-listed; CSP violations still fail). Python suite +1. **Irreversibility check:** no golden re-pin, no migration, no new `world_mutations` write path — a static file split plus one ungated GET route.

### Added
- **ADR-006 (Proposed) + ensemble action plan — the freeze challenged from the mechanics up** (`docs/decisions/ADR-006-evolving-world-with-memory.md`, `docs/roadmap/ensemble-action-plan.md`). The project owner challenged the freeze-at-launch stance directly: the goal is an *evolving* world — is the freeze truly necessary for persistence and memory? The ADR's answer, argued from verified mechanics: **no — the freeze is not fundamental; it is the integrity mechanism forced by using the generator as the world's storage medium.** The runtime regenerates the tree from the content banks on every request (`_build_world` → `generate_node_hierarchy`, no cache; no table stores nodes), and all durable history keys on `(world_seed, node_name)` — so a bank edit shifts RNG draws, renames nodes, and severs history. But the name format already separates identity from content (`<base>-<path digits>`; bank words may not contain `-`): memory needs stable identity + append-only history, **not** byte-identical regenerability. Four options laid out — A: materialize world-as-data pre-launch; **B (recommended): launch with the freeze re-worded as *scaffolding*, land an additive `nodes` mirror table verified mirror≡generation, pivot the read path post-launch, then open evolution** (banks unfreeze for new growth; deliberate change to existing nodes becomes a chronicled `WORLD_EVOLVED` event — change-as-witnessed-event serves the memory thesis better than change-made-impossible, the pattern PUZZLE_REARM already proves); C: era-gated generative evolution only; D: status quo. Status **Proposed — awaiting ratification** (interview-me practice; re-words a one-way-door covenant, so the human is the gate). The action plan sequences all eleven-persona findings into five tracks: Track 0 the evolution decision, Track 1 pre-launch gates (the `/register` CSP fix first, `FLY_API_TOKEN`, staging rehearsal), Track 2 the launch-window senses batch (hero sigil, sound invitation, pitch captures, engine-room header tucked away), Track 3 early post-launch (the pivot, evolution, cohort rhythm, since-you-left recap), Track 4 scheduled/trigger-driven — with an explicit mapping from each evaluation recommendation to its plan item (rec 3, the ceiling memo, is superseded by ADR-006: the challenge was accepted rather than documented). Docs-only; no migration, no golden re-pin, no new `world_mutations` write path.
- **Expert ensemble evaluation — eleven personas judge the player experience against its audiences** (`docs/evaluation/2026-07-19-expert-ensemble-evaluation.md`). A structured evaluation by an ensemble of expert personas — ambient-games critic, boutique-publisher scout, Claude-platform partner, systems/puzzle/narrative designers, art and audio directors, first-session and returning players, and a technical director — each run against the shipped code, with a richness/completeness scorecard across eleven experience dimensions (navigation, interaction, visual, auditory, puzzle, presence, persistence…). Everything load-bearing was re-verified this session: suite executed (789+1 skip in-sandbox; +3 `websockets` conformance tests = the advertised 792), the seed-42 world regenerated (4,439 nodes matching the freeze pins), a full puzzle census computed (**74% of all puzzles are the three decode families** — anagram 1,184 / pattern 1,159 / cipher 965; distinct answers 1,037 (~23%), "16" alone answers 109 nodes; LOCK 1,064 / lineage 517 / bond 447 / enfold 19 are the world-reading signature; 66 of 165 locked rooms don't serve the LOCK their fiction promises), bibles measured past the cache floor (18,456 / 17,825 chars), and fallback voices sampled in-register. **One new pre-launch defect found and confirmed by execution: `/register` is CSP-dead** — the page's entire registration logic is an inline `<script>` (`static/register.html:62-101`) served under `script-src 'self'` with no `unsafe-inline`/nonce (header verified against the live server), so a CSP-enforcing browser blocks the self-service invite flow silently; the E2E smoke suite covers `/` and `/app` but not `/register`. Four convergent findings (the infrastructure has outrun the presentation; `/register` is the one broken front door; the frozen puzzle ceiling is the biggest unmade decision — with an epoch-gated family hatch named; the honesty culture is an asset) and eight ranked recommendations. Docs-only; no migration, no golden re-pin, no new `world_mutations` write path.
- **ADR-005: launch-window operations policy — the four decisions between "code-ready" and "launch-ready", ratified** (`docs/decisions/ADR-005-launch-operations.md`; produced by the interview-me practice — each decision presented with options and trade-offs, all four recommendations ratified by the human on 2026-07-19):
  - **Backups go hourly; continuous replication is scheduled, not skipped** (`.github/workflows/backup.yml`, runbook §7, `phase-2-scale.md`). The off-host cadence moves from daily to hourly (hour-stamped artifacts so same-day runs can't collide; machine-local prune widened to 6), cutting the permanent chronicle's loss window 24h → 1h with zero new dependencies. Litestream-style continuous WAL replication (loss window → seconds) is recorded in the roadmap as the FIRST post-launch batch — deliberately not launch-week work, because it is a new external seam (it wants to own WAL checkpointing while the server checkpoints on SIGTERM; entrypoint and restore procedure both change) and the repo's worst shipped defects all lived at rushed external seams.
  - **The permanent world begins after a staging rehearsal** (runbook §3a, §8). A disposable `enfolded-staging` twin (same fly.toml + `deploy.sh` via `FLY_APP`, own volume) hosts the onboarding watch, WS soak, live-voice probe, and a restore rehearsal, then is destroyed before launch — first-time fumbling and any still-undiscovered write-path bug stay out of permanent history, and the deploy runbook gets exercised end-to-end before the run that counts. The §8 onboarding item now names the venue explicitly.
  - **Beta client posture: explorer default, `/app` the named alternate** (`static/guide.html`, README matrix). Invites keep landing on `/`; the guide now introduces `/app` as "the scene view" to try once oriented (the invite key carries over via localStorage — verified in `auth.js`). Not "experimental" — it is E2E-tested; finishing the parity harness over the remaining hand-mirrored explorer lines is post-launch debt paydown, not a launch gate.
  - **Voice default bumped `claude-opus-4-7` → `claude-opus-4-8`** (`consciousness._MODEL`, README, CLAUDE.md). Same price ($5/$25 per MTok), same API surface (no request-shape changes — verified against current API reference), same 4096-token prompt-cache minimum, so `_OPUS_CACHE_MIN_TOKENS` and both bibles stand unchanged; the live-voice spot-check on the new default rides the staging rehearsal's probe.
